## 2026-08-24 01:34 CST — UAT deployment paused; two auth P1 findings proven

- No commit, push, UAT configuration or deployment occurred. The implementation agent
  was paused before remote mutation while two independent read-only reviews inspected
  the local 69A.7Y2 slice.
- P1-A: only the refresh owner response can carry Kinde's request-local `Set-Cookie`
  mutations. A non-owner waiter currently treats Redis `rotated` as proof that it owns
  those cookies and redirects directly to the protected destination. Owner-response loss
  can therefore create an expired-cookie refresh loop for the retained barrier TTL. The
  existing 50-request test proves one exchange but does not model per-response cookies.
- P1-B: pinned Kinde callback code catches `State not found` internally and returns a 500
  JSON response. The outer route's `catch` is unreachable for the target error, while
  the focal suite tests only a regex helper and not the actual route/SDK behavior.
- Exact `/api` and `/api/auth` return targets also require explicit fail-closed coverage.
- P2: raw-path checks also permit dot-segment forms that the browser normalizes into a
  forbidden auth route. Return targets must be canonicalized before root checks.
- P1-C: the concrete refresh route constructs the Kinde session, reads HMAC config and
  creates the Redis barrier before the pure handler rejects invalid Sec-Fetch headers.
  The existing test exercises only the pure handler, so it does not prove cross-site
  requests fail before Kinde/config/Redis.
- Gate 69A.7Y2B replaces 69A.7Y3 temporarily. It is a local-only correction with
  route/SDK-shaped tests and distinct owner/waiter cookie-delivery modeling. Providers,
  Advisor, remotes, deployment, real refresh, commit/push and product/serving writes are
